## 6. Owner's own steps (copy of OWNER_STEPS.md — do NOT do these; they wait for him)
# Шаги владельца (в самом конце, после кода)

Делаем по порядку. На каждом шаге я подскажу, что нажать.
Секреты (ключи, ссылки) в чат не присылать: кладём их в AWS Secrets Manager.

## 1. Деплой
1. Я отменю старые зависшие запуски деплоя.
2. Ты нажимаешь «Approve» на новом деплое в GitHub.

## 2. Cloudflare
1. Ключи TURN и SFU (звонки), затем проверяем звонки на двух телефонах.
2. Перенос DNS на Cloudflare (`docs/runbooks/DNS_TO_CLOUDFLARE.md`).
3. Настройка R2 (картинки и видео), затем переключение.
4. Ключи пакета C (Turnstile и др., `docs/runbooks/batch-c-owner.md`).

## 3. Тревоги на телефон — PagerDuty (бесплатно)
Инструкция: `docs/runbooks/paging.md`.
1. Регистрация на pagerduty.com, план Free.
2. Приложение PagerDuty на iPhone, разрешить «критические уведомления».
3. Сервис «LawBid prod», интеграция «Amazon CloudWatch», скопировать ссылку.
4. Ссылку положить в секрет `lawbid-prod/terraform-tfvars`, затем Run.

## 4. AWS
1. Потолок бюджета: удалить старый бюджет на $50.
2. Включить теги расходов (Billing → Cost allocation tags).
3. Bootstrap-applies: роль для ночной проверки и права для учебных аварий. Затем секрет `AWS_PLAN_ROLE` в GitHub.
4. После первого подписанного деплоя включить `SIGN_REQUIRED`.
5. Одобрить зеркало ClamAV (антивирус файлов).
6. ARM64 и Spot, когда скажу, что готово (`docs/runbooks/ARM64_SPOT.md`).

## 4b. Права для админки (после вливания PR #339)
1. Один Run с планом (plan-guard.sh): права для PagerDuty, бюджета и страницы «Расходы».
2. После этого в админке заработают кнопки PagerDuty, бюджета и цифры AWS.

## 5. Twilio
1. Гео-разрешения: только США.
2. Включить автопополнение баланса ($50–100).

## 6. Нагрузочные тесты
1. Сначала локально (делаю я).
2. Потом на временном стенде AWS (Run от тебя, стенд удаляется после).

## 7. Сборки и магазины
1. Свежие сборки на iPhone и Android.
2. Файл .aab для Google Play.
3. Apple: оформить LLC-аккаунт разработчика.
4. Stripe: живой ключ, цена подписки ($99, цена основателя, годовая).

## 7b. Юридическое (без кода)
- MFA на root-аккаунт AWS и удалить его ключи.
- Зарегистрировать DMCA-агента в US Copyright Office ($6, раз в 3 года).
- Налоговые регистрации по штатам (Нью-Йорк облагает подписки).
- Юрист по этике: платные ставки, рефералы, комиссия (NY 7.2 / 5.4).
- Включить правила веток в GitHub (скрипт подготовлю).

## 8. Позже
- Страховка от утечек (Vouch или Embroker), примерно $1,5–3 тыс. в год.
- Один раз показать схему юристу по этике в Нью-Йорке.
- Пентест, когда появятся первые серьёзные клиенты.

Owner 2026-10-22 (after PR #236 merged). Groups/channels stay for the next update; gifts hidden (code kept). Texts en+ru only. One build per batch; test on emulator/simulator first, then the real iPhone (cable) and Samsung.

1. Calls like WhatsApp: bottom bar Speaker · Video · Mute · End; switch audio↔video mid-call, the peer gets an in-call prompt WITH a sound («X turned on video — turn on yours?» on/off); 1:1 and group calls in chats (≤9), not channels. Avatars on the call screen (fallback to initials on load error). Video layout like Zoom: expand one participant full screen and back, a button next to × to expand any participant. Polls inside group calls (plan 26b items 10–11). Hide the gifts system (keep code).
2. Feed: swipe between Posts | Reels | Cases must continue into Chats and back.
3. Reels upload: no «Загрузка 0% / Отмена» top bar; like Instagram — fast, background, progressive (check what #233 already did).
4. The «Сервер LawBid сейчас недоступен / Повторить» and «Снова в сети» banners must never be shown to users — remove.
5. Chat media bubbles: a photo/video/link sent shows the raw encrypted body `enc:v2.m1…` under the image — fix; media messages like Instagram/WhatsApp (image + time overlay, no raw text).
6. Contact info screen (Данные контакта): verify every row works (Media/links/docs, mute, disappearing, mute calls, pin, chat note) and the cosmetics match the source app (WhatsApp/Telegram).
7. Search fields close on a tap in empty space (not only the keyboard).
8. Chat «+» button (new chat): like WhatsApp's FAB — a noticeable colour per theme.
9. Push notifications like WhatsApp: sender avatar + saved name / username as the title, the message as the body (not «LawBid / Новое сообщение»). Server payload + Android/iOS styling.

10. Zoom-like expansion = in GROUP video calls (≤9): tap a participant → their video full screen, back to the grid.
11. Stories: hide everything for now (keep code): «add to story» in the share sheet, the stories tray in chats; a tap on an avatar opens the avatar (photo), not a story. Both roles.
12. Call «add participant»: not the «@username» box — a WhatsApp-style panel sliding in from the right: contacts + people from chats (anyone with a conversation, even unfollowed) + mutual follows, contacts/chats first, search by name/username within them; strangers not callable here.
13. App-icon badge counter: an on/off switch in the bell screen's settings AND mirrored 1:1 in Profile → Settings → Notifications (inbox 29/29b/29c; local branch claude/badge-toggle-29 + worktree ab96bb have unfinished work).
14. Q&A tabs (Все · Мои · Лучшие · Без ответа): the client's are cramped — make them wider / centred like the attorney's. Always fix both roles.

15. Chats: hide the channels (megaphone) icon entirely (code kept); the «+» becomes a bottom-right FAB like WhatsApp; the freed filter row = Instagram-style equal pill tabs full width (Все · Кейсы · Ожидают · Запросы ›).
16. Notifications screen: no section chips (Все/Важные/Остальные/Помощники); header right side = bell · filter · magnifier, close together.
17. Chat note sheet: «Изменить» left, «Открыть чат» right (one row), «Удалить» below.

18. Team tab (Чаты · Команда): add an «assistant roles» icon next to the team-settings icon, open to EVERY attorney: shows all assistant capabilities (what seats give); toggles work normally with seats; without seats a toggle offers to buy assistant seats (upsell, like Slack/Notion).
19. BILLING BUG: buying assistant seats («количество помощников → сохранить») only charges next month, so seats can't be used at once. Required: buy seats immediately (prorated charge now, Stripe subscription-item quantity with proration/immediate invoice), then the seat price rides in the monthly subscription charge; admin panel updated if needed. Big system — research Stripe + our subscriptions module, then plan + questions before code.

20. The chats «+» screen: three tabs in this order — Чаты (people I chat with + mutual follows, from my_people.dart) · Контакты · Звонки; opens on Чаты.
Billing decisions (owner 2026-10-22): seat bought = prorated charge NOW (like Slack); seat removed = works until period end, no refund/credit; seats only after the first paid subscription period (not in trial). Max duties per assistant: default 3, editable in admin (goal: more assistants, no one gets the whole account).
21. Payments UX: subscriptions are paid on the WEBSITE in our styled card (no App Store / Play commission — multiplatform service); the previous session removed Apple Pay / Google Pay from that card — restore them (Stripe on web); make the same styled card for every other payment (seats, boosts…). No in-app purchase flows. BUG: boost/promotion checkout sends to Stripe's hosted page; after abandoning it the post shows «Завершить оплату» with no way to cancel or restart (the last step just reopens the same page) — needs our styled card + cancel/restart.

Release step (owner 2026-10-22): after the cosmetics merge → push + PR + merge (API deploys to AWS on merge) → install PROD-flavor builds (api.lawbid.dev) on the Samsung and the iPhone, remove the old dev builds and the Oct-19 prod build on the emulator so nothing stale gets released by mistake. Owner reported on the dev build: contacts not loading inside «+», photo/video uploads failing — likely the local server being off; re-check on prod.

22. Settings → «Очистить кэш» like Telegram: removes only on-device copies of media/images/thumbnails (show size per category, keep data: chats, messages, account); re-entering a chat re-downloads and re-caches. Part of the Telegram-style economy: on-device caches of frequently opened things (chats, avatars of frequent contacts, media), fewer server hits, stable connection, smart auto-refresh — study how Telegram/WhatsApp/Instagram do it and propose internal/external solutions (quality + economy, never hangs, never loses the server link). Owner (later the same day): also ADMIN controls for this (default cache limit, keep-media defaults, auto-download defaults); on reaching the limit the device auto-evicts old media (server keeps everything); re-entering a chat auto-downloads per the auto-download settings (photos/videos/files by Wi-Fi / mobile) or by tap on the file/photo/video — exactly like Telegram; study Telegram thoroughly first; goal: no storage losses for us, app never hangs. Owner: build it EXACTLY like Telegram's cache architecture (limits included: Telegram's «Использование памяти» — max cache size slider, keep-media duration per chat type, auto-remove by LRU, clear by category). Caches must be COMPRESSED but good quality (Telegram model: streamed video in chunks, small thumbnails, size cap with LRU auto-cleanup, user-set «keep media N days / max N GB») — a single video must never eat gigabytes of the user's storage.

23. Contacts sync like WhatsApp/Telegram: after the first full upload send only DELTAS (added/removed numbers, hashed), server-side matching stays; part of the economy batch. (#238 fixed the breakage but still uploads the whole book each sync.)

**Why:** the owner's pre-release cosmetics pass. **How to apply:** research the reference app first, one PR per item, cheap models for mechanical parts, strong for calls/media. See [[owner-working-rules]].

- 2026-10-23 night: docs/perf/BIG_PLATFORM_ARCHITECTURE_2026-10-23.md written (sonnet, web links from memory — verify before citing). CONNECTION FLAPPING ROOT CAUSES: (1) access token TTL 900 s, server emits auth:expired + closes socket (realtime.gateway.ts:726-739), app refreshes only on 401 → socket dies every 15 min; (2) every reconnect triggers ~10 REST refetches (no event seq / connectionStateRecovery); (3) deploy/scale-in: stopTimeout 30 s + deregistration 30 s, client reconnect 1–10 s little jitter; (4) reachability marks offline after one failure; (5) phone banner already removed 25c4e6238 (owner's build is older); (6) admin has no banner/socket — likely dev-server restarts; admin proxy fetch has no timeout/try-catch, retries without backoff. ### memory: fresh-research.md
---
name: fresh-research
description: Research must use FRESH sources (2025-2026) via real web search; never rely on memory or old practices
metadata:
  type: feedback
---
Every worker brief must say to research with WebSearch/WebFetch (mode extended for niche items) and to prefer sources from 2025-2026: current docs, recent engineering blogs, current prices. Note the date of each source in the report. If web search is unavailable, the worker must say so, and the coordinator re-runs the research.
**Why:** the owner (2026-10-24) said to study fresh things, not old; the benchmark worker wrote from memory without searching.
**How to apply:** add the line "Research with live web search, prefer 2025-2026 sources, cite dates" to every research or build brief. Related: [[research-first-workflow]]

### memory: local-dev-environment.md
---
name: local-dev-environment
description: "How the local LawBid stack and test devices are wired on the Mac (API watch mode, logs, devices, web logins)"
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: reference
  originSessionId: 89489739-0c64-4b11-8f30-2213648918ab
  modified: 2026-10-21T15:53:26.871Z
---

- Local API runs as `nest start --watch` (apps/api) → auto-restarts on source edits; stdout/stderr go to `/tmp/lb_api.log` (pretty pino, grep `WARN (` lines to find real errors the owner hit on the phones).
- DB: docker `lawbid-postgres-1`, user `root`, db `lawbid` (`docker exec lawbid-postgres-1 psql -U root -d lawbid`). e2e make their own `<db>_e2e_*` DBs; run with `E2E_ISOLATION=<tag> E2E_REDIS_DB=<n>` to not clash with parallel runs.
- Devices (2026-10-21): Samsung `R5GL11433DD` = Thomas (attorney), emulator `emulator-5554` = Charlotte (client); adb at `~/Library/Android/sdk/platform-tools/adb`. Package `com.lawbid.lawbid.dev`. iPhone install: `flutter build ios --release --flavor dev …` then `xcrun devicectl device install app`.
- API calls as a user without kicking the phone: password login with platform `web` and the user's existing web deviceId (Thomas `dev-att1`, Charlotte `dev-char1`, Piter `dev-hb-14155552681`). See [[owner-working-rules]].
- Agent worktrees (`isolation: worktree`) may start from `main`, not the release head — tell agents to reset onto the release head first.

### memory: no-second-admin.md
---
name: no-second-admin
description: Owner forbids four-eyes / second-admin approvals; one 2FA code is enough for every admin action
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: feedback
  originSessionId: 6d6b8c8c-3cb5-4d35-85a9-f97482dd18a3
  modified: 2026-10-25T06:58:19.993Z
---

Never design admin actions that need a second admin's approval (four-eyes / maker-checker). The owner works alone and runs the company; a fresh 2FA code from the acting admin is the only confirmation. Dual control stays only as a setting `admin.dual_control`, default off.

**Why:** 2026-10-25 the owner said «Убери эту вещь вообще с корня, не хочу чтобы ограничивал сам себя… я же один работаю» — with one admin, four-eyes buttons could never be confirmed.

**How to apply:** in any new admin feature (ops buttons, money actions), use RBAC + 2FA step-up + audit log, no approval queue. Related: [[owner-working-rules]], [[scaling-principle]].

### memory: owner-language.md
---
name: owner-language
description: "Always talk to the owner in Russian, plain words, as to a non-programmer"
metadata:
  type: user
---

The owner (SiMA) is not a programmer. Every reply, question, recap and next-step box: in Russian, plain simple words, no jargon (no "fast-forward", "stash", "patch", etc. — explain what it means for him instead). Short. Related: [[owner-working-rules]].

### memory: owner-short-answers.md
---
name: owner-short-answers
description: Owner wants very short, simple answers — 2-4 lines, no tables or long lists unless asked
metadata:
  type: feedback
---
Answer the owner in 2-4 short plain Russian lines. No long tables, no "books", no tech words.
**Why:** 2026-10-24 he got angry twice: long texts he could not understand; he praised a 3-line answer ("мог бы так коротко и ясно").
**How to apply:** lead with the direct answer; add detail only if he asks. Related: [[owner-language]]

### memory: owner-working-rules.md
---
name: owner-working-rules
description: "How the LawBid owner (SiMA) wants every change done — whole system, all languages, ask on big systems, local only"
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: feedback
  originSessionId: ba9f5968-93fa-47c6-ab08-8c709a67902f
  modified: 2026-10-22T01:56:49.114Z
---

- A request about one spot = rework the whole related system (e.g. "subscribe button" → the whole subscriptions system). If the system is big, ASK the owner how to implement it first. **Why:** owner was unhappy with single-button fixes. **How to apply:** scope every task to its system; post a short question with options for large ones.
- Languages (owner 2026-10-21, replaces the 2026-10-18 "all languages at once" rule): for now new/changed texts go in en + ru only. When the owner says the project is finished, translate all new keys into the other languages in the seed file. Keep a list of en+ru-only keys (e.g. in the journal) so none is missed.
- GitHub (owner 2026-10-21, replaces the old "local only" rule): commit finished tasks and push; I merge PRs myself once everything is done, checked and working (CI green, tested on devices). gh is logged in as syimykkalykov1999 (push/admin on LawBid). Don't ask about things that change nothing (e.g. commit author email). See [[research-first-workflow]].
- Devices (owner 2026-10-21): allowed without asking — local services, builds, his Android phone (install + test), Android emulator, iOS simulator.
- Theme tokens only, no gold/yellow fills; no SMS; reply in Russian, short. See docs/HANDOFF.md for the rest.
- Sub-agent models (owner 2026-10-18): simple mechanical tasks (translations, renames, code search) → cheap models; hard ones (architecture, multi-file edits, debugging) → strong models. Never send a model too weak to finish (rework wastes the same tokens). Parallel threads OK, no waste. Verify cheap models' work yourself.
- Translations: all 85 languages live in apps/api/prisma/seed/translations_seed.xlsx; add with scripts/i18n-add.mjs, check with scripts/i18n-audit.mjs.
- Questions / choices / permission asks to the owner: post in the thread AND send a copy to the coordinator (send_message @parent) so it reaches the project chat; never choose for him (2026-10-18, after he missed the stickers question).
- Production already runs on AWS with every third-party service (AWS, Stripe, Firebase, Bunny…) configured in the production admin. Never change integrations, keys, env or provider logic; local work = updates/fixes only (owner 2026-10-18).
- RELEASE ORDER (owner 2026-10-19): 1 push fresh code to GitHub (with him) → 2 rotate keys in every service → 3 deploy to AWS → 4 Play Store + App Store → 5 motion/video promo → ads. Don't drift to other work first; details in docs/HANDOFF.md «RELEASE PLAN».
- 2026-10-19 night: owner froze the code («сам код не трогай») — no code edits, commits or builds in this thread unless he asks here; GitHub/PR/CI/release is done by another session.
- 2026-10-21 release freeze: everything merged into claude/release-20oct up to 60ae65d8 must not be changed or "improved" — EXCEPT the owner's inbox (~/Desktop/LawBid-download/inbox/notes.txt + screens): handoff 13.99–13.101 say inbox items are his word and rank above the freeze; do them without asking, minimal diff. Final list must name every file changed after 60ae65d8 (kept in docs/runbooks/RELEASE_20OCT_FILES.md).
- Local device testing (2026-10-21): demo password `Demo1234!` (password/login); a second login of the same user as platform `web` with an existing web deviceId doesn't kick the phone. Release APKs must be re-signed with ~/.android/debug.keystore (apksigner + Android Studio JBR java) to install over the phones' builds without data loss.
- Builds (owner 2026-10-21): build only when sure the system is complete, working and well-architected — no trial builds.
- All roles (owner 2026-10-21): any fix/change applies everywhere the same thing exists — attorney, client, assistant, admin/web — not only the profile where he noticed it. Applies to everything, not just buttons.
- Mac disk cleanup (owner 2026-10-21): he watches disk space himself; clean unneeded files ONLY when he says «нужна очистка».
- Scale (owner 2026-10-22): LawBid is NOT an MVP — design every change for millions (even billions) of users: caching, rate limits, CDN, queues, cost controls so AWS bills stay sane; reference architectures Instagram/WhatsApp/Telegram/WeChat. After the cosmetics + release: a dedicated server scalability/cost audit (DB indexes, hot-path caching, autoscaling, SFU for big group video) before changing anything.
- Context hygiene (owner 2026-10-22): keep state in memory files (backlog, rules) so compaction loses nothing; clean Mac build caches when he reminds.
- Infra economy (owner 2026-10-22): servers must autoscale up under load and DOWN when quiet (pay only for use); Telegram-style client-side economy — compressed data and on-device caches of frequently opened things, so the app hits the server less; maximum economy without losing quality or availability. Part of the post-release scalability/cost audit.
- Audit mandate (owner 2026-10-22): find EVERY badly designed thing (by him or earlier sessions) across app, API, admin, web, infra; compare each with the best platforms (Telegram/WhatsApp/Instagram), pick the best, and fix them all — research → plan → code → check, in priority order. Report lives in docs/perf/SERVER_LOAD_AUDIT_2026-10-22.md.
- Initiative (owner 2026-10-22): «if I didn't mention something, it means I don't know it» — proactively secure/improve internal and external systems (security, cost, reliability) even unasked; small safe things: do and report; big things or anything touching payments, access, keys, prod: short plan + his yes first.
- Questions (owner 2026-10-22): he may miss questions in chat — ask them LOUDLY (short separate message, options as buttons/numbered choices, at the top), remind up to 3 times; after the 3rd unanswered reminder choose the best option myself and say what I chose and why.
- Architecture (owner 2026-10-22): scalable AND isolated — each subsystem (chats, calls, feed, billing, push, contacts, search) scales on its own and a failure in one must not break the others (queues, per-module rate limits/timeouts, circuit breakers, no cross-module synchronous fan-out); also hunt SQL/command injection and any internal/external system that drains money or slows the API/app/web/admin.
- Rebuild mandate (owner 2026-10-22): «build solid architecture; if what was there before is bad, change whatever you need — allowed». Replace poor designs outright (keep behaviour for users, migrate data safely); for big rebuilds touching money/access/prod/user data give a short plan first, then go.
- Token economy (owner 2026-10-22 night): on any error/problem search the web and reference architectures FIRST, then code — never guess by trial and error; use proven libraries instead of writing code ourselves; clean Mac build caches after each finished worker; max 2 workers in parallel; no questions to him while he sleeps — decide by the rules, collect truly-owner-only items (Stripe, Terraform, Xcode, passwords) into one list for later.
- Libraries (owner 2026-10-22): only reliable, well-maintained, widely used ones (official SDKs; popular packages with active maintenance) — never obscure/abandoned packages.
- Builds need his explicit OK (owner 2026-10-22 night): no device/app builds at all until he reads the morning summary and says so.
- No repeats (owner 2026-10-22 night): every new audit/fix brief must list what is already fixed (the DONE lines in cosmetics-backlog) and search only for NEW issues; never re-audit or re-fix the same thing.
- Pre-release mandate (owner 2026-10-22 evening): after the audit-4 PRs, rework EVERY client↔server data flow Telegram/Instagram/WhatsApp-style (deltas, events, local cache with limits, «Очистить кэш»): likes, comments, follows/subscriptions, feed, reels, Q&A, profiles, cases, notifications, search — and everything else found in the app, "all to the last one"; goal: server never falls, no freezes, no third-party service drains money, no hack/data theft. Also research how apps get hacked (past and now) and harden; evaluate the blogger's claim "send data separately from code" (secrets/data plane separation) and apply what is real. Then APK/iOS builds ONLY on his command → release.
- Token economy, reinforced (owner 2026-10-22 evening): «не трать мои токены впустую на вещи, которые вообще не нужны». Only work that is needed: no redundant checks, no re-reading, no decorative outputs (mockups, long reports) unless asked; short replies; one worker per task, not duplicates; stop polling — wait for notifications.
- Order change (owner 2026-10-22 night): do NOT rush to the green theme / translations / builds. After the current economy + security work, first walk through EVERY internal and external system step by step with him (checklist: each subsystem vs Instagram/TikTok/WhatsApp/Telegram — what is done, what is missing, "down to the small things"), fix what's missing, and only once he is convinced we match the big services → builds, languages, themes.
- Free hand on architecture (owner 2026-10-22 night, reconfirmed): the internal/external systems "appeared by themselves" while building the apps — if anything old blocks speed, load capacity or economy, change/replace it as planned without asking. Load & speed are a standing priority.
- Architecture method (owner 2026-10-22 night): for each system, study how the best services do it (Telegram/WhatsApp/Instagram/TikTok/Stripe…), pick the best design, then build that same architecture here carefully, without errors — replace ours boldly where it differs.
- i18n in practice (2026-10-22 night): CI step «Translations are complete in every language» (scripts/i18n-audit.mjs --ci) fails on any empty cell, so every new key must be added with ALL 85 languages (machine-translate via a cheap agent at PR time: produce a JSON for scripts/i18n-add.mjs from the xlsx header languages). The owner's "en+ru only" rule now means: en+ru are authoritative, the rest are machine drafts reviewed in the final translation pass.
- Ready-made first (owner 2026-10-22 night, reconfirmed): before building any system, search GitHub / docs for proven, maintained ready solutions (libraries, services, reference implementations: e.g. LiveKit/mediasoup for SFU, official SDKs), adapt them to us — never build a whole system from scratch when a good one exists.
- Mac cleanup cadence (owner 2026-10-23, reinforced "не забывай очищать мак"): after every finished worker remove its worktree (if the branch is merged) and build caches; periodically clear ~/.gradle/caches and ~/.npm/_cacache when disk < ~20 GB; keep one worktree with a real npm ci install as the shared node_modules source.
- Hosting (owner 2026-10-23): stay on AWS permanently — no migration to Hetzner/DO etc. Cost cuts only inside AWS (ARM, Savings Plans, Spot, Bunny CDN for media) and optional cheap hosts only for LiveKit/TURN later if he asks.
- MODEL PER AGENT IS MANDATORY (owner 2026-10-23, angry: "в двух агентах фейбл и в одном опус… ты меня грабишь"): every Agent launch MUST pass `model` explicitly — haiku for mechanical work (translations, CI-log reading, test fixture fixes, searches, renames, cleanup), sonnet for ordinary implementation and research, opus/fable ONLY for a genuinely hard architecture/multi-system piece, one at a time, and say so. Never leave the default (inherits my model). Prefer doing small fixes myself over spawning an agent.
- Handoff state (owner 2026-10-23): keep the current state in memory (cosmetics-backlog "STATE" lines: open PRs, running workers with ids/branches, next step) after every milestone, in 1–3 short lines, so another session/AI can continue from the same point — cheap, not verbose.
- Admin naming (owner 2026-10-23): in the admin, technical names of keys/settings/integrations must be shown in their ORIGINAL English term (e.g. «LIVEKIT_API_KEY», «Twilio Account SID», «Stripe webhook secret») next to the Russian label — he lost an hour looking for a key that was translated. Every integration page gets a short step-by-step «как включить» block (where to click, which key goes where). LiveKit: make enabling it a one-click admin action if feasible (API triggers the infra switch), otherwise the simplest documented steps.
- RELEASE SEQUENCE (owner 2026-10-23): when the current work is in main → builds on his Samsung + iPhone and live test together → deploy to AWS (terraform apply + API/admin/web deploy, with him) → store submissions (Play + App Store). Builds only when he says he is at the laptop.
- APPLE ACCOUNT (2026-10-23): Apple Developer Support case 102987354798 (Scott) agreed to migrate the individual membership (Team ID 78T56RPM74) to SiMA LLC (Illinois, D-U-N-S 129389148, site lawbid.dev). During migration the Certificates/Identifiers/Profiles portal is unavailable → ORDER: 1) with owner at laptop: portal/Xcode setup (App Attest, associated domains webcredentials + applinks, NSE target for push avatars) + device builds; 2) owner replies "Ready to start the migration"; 3) test + AWS deploy during migration; 4) App Store submission after migration. Google Play: create an ORGANIZATION account for SiMA LLC (avoids the 20-tester/14-day rule for personal accounts). Todo: add «© SiMA LLC» to the lawbid.dev home footer (LawBidSite repo).
- PRE-STORE RULE (owner 2026-10-23, after the 12 MB uncompressed web bundle): anything that breaks/slows/costs at scale must be found and fixed BEFORE store release — a fix needing an app update costs days of store review. Prefer server-side/remote-config fixes; every client limit/interval/kill switch must come from app_config so future fixes need no app update. Scale-limits audit worker afb239f3b3b219026 (sonnet) running; PR #272 = web gzip fix.
- BIG-PLATFORM SERVER ARCHITECTURE MANDATE (owner 2026-10-23 night): study how Instagram/Telegram/WhatsApp/TikTok (and others he doesn't know) run their server side — compression, economy, load distribution, balancers, connection handling, data transfer — "all of it, including what I don't know", and build the same here without mistakes. Also: the app loses and restores the server connection (connectivity flapping) and the admin does the same — he calls it unacceptable; must be diagnosed and fixed (root cause, not a banner).
- SENIOR-DEV RULE (owner 2026-10-23, hurt: "ты работаешь так, если я не скажу — ты не делаешь"): act as the senior engineer who KNOWS what a product needs — never wait for the owner to name a problem. Before declaring anything "ready": run the big-platform readiness pass yourself (compression, payload/asset sizes, connection lifecycle incl. token expiry, reconnect/backoff, deploy without drops, limits, data growth, caches, cost) across app + web + admin + API + infra, and fix what you find. Missing this twice (12 MB web bundle, 15-min socket drop) is the reference failure to avoid.
- OWNER MANDATE (2026-10-23, after the web-compression and socket-drop misses): I am the senior engineer, architect and foreman; he is not a programmer. For EVERY subsystem (app, web, admin, API, infra, external services) study how the big platforms (Instagram, Telegram, WhatsApp, TikTok, Slack, Discord, Stripe…) build it — "all of it, down to the last thing", including what he doesn't know to ask — then build the same here, before touching code. Proactively TELL him the things "without which we're dead" (like compression, token lifecycle) before they bite; never wait to be asked. Study → architecture → code, every time.
- NO "GOOD ENOUGH FOR LAUNCH" FRAMING (owner 2026-10-23, angry): never justify a gap with "fine until launch / until we grow". Instead: build every growth step NOW as a ready switch (Terraform var / app_config), and keep a GROWTH LADDER doc (10k→100k→1M→10M→100M+): per step what to flip, who, minutes, cost, and the monitoring signal that says "now". Only COST decides when a switch is flipped, never missing code. Client (store) side must never need an update for scale. Task queued: docs/perf/GROWTH_LADDER + switches worker after the 3 running workers.
- MAC HYGIENE (owner 2026-10-23, after the Mac + Docker hung): clean the Mac OFTEN (after every finished worker and every few hours: build outputs, merged worktrees, simulator junk, stale caches) and run ONLY what the current step needs — start Docker/local API/emulators when a step needs them, stop them as soon as work moves elsewhere (e.g. cloud/CI work → Docker off). Max 2 heavy workers at a time. Owner asleep, Mac on power + Wi-Fi: keep working the plan; questions at the end.
- PROPOSE PROACTIVELY (owner 2026-10-23): "if I don't know it, that doesn't mean don't tell me or don't do it — if it's a plus for the project, propose it WITH the 'because'". Infrastructure must be built properly. Audit measurements at BILLION-user scale (model/extrapolate), not thousands. Phone builds postponed until infra/architecture work is done (he'll reconnect phones). Questions only at the end.
- NIGHT MANDATE (owner 2026-10-23 ~06:40 PDT, asleep): work alone by the rules — find/fix/research what the best platforms do, copy proven ready solutions, code only with a blueprint; merge via GitHub and deploy to AWS through the Deploy workflow (owner approves the production gate when awake — queue deploys, don't wait on him); check the web app after deploy. Owner-only items (Namecheap DNS fix, Apple, etc.) go to the morning list. I am the chief architect; cheap models for easy tasks, capable ones for hard ones; results over speed.
- MAC CLEANUP SCHEDULE (owner 2026-10-23 07:15 PDT): cleanup every 2 hours (cron in session: build outputs, merged worktrees, caches if <15 GB free, stop unneeded services) + after every finished worker. Owner asleep; morning list only.

## Модель самого прораба (2026-10-23, владелец зол второй раз)
- Fable у владельца упёрся в лимит. Сама сессия-прораб (эта) тоже должна жить на Sonnet, а не на Fable/Opus: ожидание CI, merge, чтение уведомлений — дешёвая работа. Fable/Opus — только если владелец сам выбрал в меню модели для конкретной сложной задачи.
- Воркеры: haiku — механика (переводы, фикстуры, переименования), sonnet — обычный код, сильная модель — только по явному слову владельца. Проверять модель в каждом Agent-вызове явно.
- **Why:** токены = деньги владельца; дважды поймал на дорогих моделях.
- **How to apply:** при старте сессии, если модель Fable/Opus, сразу сказать владельцу переключить меню на Sonnet (сам сессия себя переключить не может).

## Нагрузочные тесты (решение владельца 2026-10-23)
- Согласен на 2 теста перед релизом: «1 млн DAU» (~$10–30) и «10 млн DAU» (~$50–150), через GitHub workflow «Load test» (OIDC, без ключей на Mac), с одобрением как у Deploy.
- **Обязательно:** тестовая копия удаляется сама (destroy в `always()`), все ресурсы с тегом `lawbid-loadtest`, отдельная проверка-«сторож» по расписанию удаляет забытое старше 4 ч, бюджет-алерт AWS; после каждого теста показать владельцу доказательство, что ничего не осталось. Перед запуском назвать точную цену.
- **Why:** владелец не хочет платить за забытую копию.
- Нагрузочные тесты — по-взрослому, чтобы не платить за неудачные прогоны: (1) бесплатный прогон на Mac в Docker ловит ошибки настройки; (2) «дымовой» на AWS 5–10 мин малой нагрузкой (~$1) — проверить, что всё подключено; (3) полный прогон с автоостановкой (ошибки >5% или p95 >2 с → стоп и удаление); (4) жёсткий потолок времени (2 ч) и числа серверов на прогон; (5) чинить между прогонами локально, полный прогон только после зелёного дымового. Владелец 2026-10-23: «не хочу платить за каждый неуспешный тест».
- AWS-уборка: правила автоудаления старого (ECR, v/<sha>/ веба, версии S3, логи, снапшоты) — воркер a79cbbb3, док docs/runbooks/AWS_CLEANUP.md.

## Чистка worktree — сначала проверить неотслеживаемые файлы (урок 2026-10-23)
- Перед `git worktree remove --force` и любой чисткой проверять `git -C <wt> status --ignored --short` на файлы, которых нет на GitHub: `*.tfvars`, `.env`, `key.properties`, `*.jks`, `*.p8`, `config/*.json`. Если есть — сначала скопировать в надёжное место.
- Прод `terraform.tfvars` хранится в AWS Secrets Manager (`lawbid-prod/terraform-tfvars`), не только на Mac.
- **Why:** 2026-10-23 потерян prod terraform.tfvars (вероятно, при моей чистке worktree); владелец был зол.

## Решение владельца по AWS (2026-10-24): «на все да»
1) api_max_tasks 50 / worker_max_tasks 20; 2) RDS Multi-AZ + бэкапы 14 дней; 3) redis_replicas = 1; 4) turn_second_enabled + t4g.small (ночью, поэтапно); 5) uptime_check = "route53"; 6) security baseline, GuardDuty, CloudTrail, CDN WAF, backup copy в другой регион. Применять поэтапно: bootstrap-loadtest → безопасные добавления → ECS → TURN ночью.

## Защита — максимальная (владелец 2026-10-24)
- Адвокаты работают с конфиденциальными документами клиентов → защита уровня Clio/MyCase/Box (ABA 1.6, SOC 2). Воркер sonnet a38e5a00: аудит пути документов, починка кода, Terraform (Security Hub, Inspector, Macie, Vault Lock, логи), док docs/runbooks/SECURITY_LEGAL.md. Владельцу: пентест, SOC 2, страховка — позже.
- Новая таблица в Prisma → сразу `npm run db:erd --workspace apps/api` и коммит docs/db/ERD.md (CI «ERD is up to date» падал дважды: #293, #296). Писать это в бриф воркерам.

## ЗАМОРОЗКА ОБЪЁМА ДО РЕЛИЗА (2026-10-24, владелец устал)
До релиза только: (1) Deploy #180 → Run prod-a (надёжность) → дымовой + «1 млн» тест → фиксы только найденного; (2) финальная сборка iOS/Android + осмотр владельцем; (3) бумаги: Apple перенос на SiMA LLC, Google Play орг-аккаунт, страницы магазинов (тексты/скриншоты готовлю я), Stripe вебхук/Apple Pay/Tax, Firebase App Check, цены $199/$50; (4) отправка в сторы.
ПОСЛЕ релиза: тест 10 млн и звонков, TURN upsize, opt-in защиты, экран «кто открывал», passkey-подсказка, память iPhone, звонки каналов, пентест/SOC2/страховка, План роста. Новое — только в «после релиза», без слова владельца не добавлять.
- ПОПРАВКА 2026-10-24: тест «10 млн» и тест звонков — ДО релиза. Уже готово у владельца: Apple аккаунт (публиковались раньше; только перенос на SiMA LLC), Google Play аккаунт (скрины и тексты загружены, ждёт .aab), Stripe (переключить на боевой ключ), Firebase подключён, цены в админке выставлены. НЕ перечислять это как задачи. Пентест/SOC2/страховка — потом.

## Не путать (владелец был в ярости 2026-10-24)
- ECS: работает 1 api + 1 worker + 1 admin; «4/2» и «50/20» — ПОТОЛОК автомасштаба (max_capacity, Terraform его игнорирует, меняется в админке «Экономика»). Всегда говорить «работает N, потолок M».
- Вход по Face ID/passkey УЖЕ есть (ссылка на экране входа); новое — только подсказка адвокату подключить.
- Кеш как в Telegram (диск) уже сделан; perf-тест мерил RAM — утечки нет, закрыто.
- Каналы в «Идёт сейчас» — следующее обновление; пентест/SOC2 — потом; журналы/Security Hub/Macie — когда появятся пользователи.
- TURN менять СЕЙЧАС (пользователей нет), не ночью.
- Экран «кто открывал документ»: клиенты И адвокаты, только документы дел; локальное хранение + дельта-синк + сжатие, как остальные данные.
- Перед ответом сверяться с памятью и кодом, не предлагать уже сделанное.
- 2026-10-24 owner: after installing builds on the phones, delete the build outputs (apps/mobile/build) to keep the Mac free; clean the Mac regularly so it does not freeze.
- 2026-10-25: the Mac FROZE when two pre-commit lints (type-aware eslint with a 12 GB heap) ran at once while 2 workers ran tests, Docker and the iOS simulator. RULES:
  - never run two commits or lints in parallel;
  - shut down the simulator when it is not needed;
  - heavy work at most 1 worker plus 1 coordinator action at a time;
  - check `memory_pressure` before heavy steps.
- 2026-10-25 owner: HEAVY jobs (jest suites, tsc, type-aware eslint/commits, docker, next/flutter builds) run ONE at a time on the Mac; LIGHT jobs (research, web search, grep/read, gh/CI watching, docs writing) run SEVERAL in parallel so he does not wait forever. Workers share a lock: wrap heavy commands in `until mkdir /tmp/lawbid-heavy.lock 2>/dev/null; do sleep 15; done; <cmd>; rmdir /tmp/lawbid-heavy.lock`.
- 2026-10-25 owner (refined): run 1 heavy + 4-5 light jobs at the same time; never 2 heavy.
- 2026-10-25 owner: Mac has 16 GB RAM; keep total use under that (check memory_pressure before heavy steps), clean regularly; research the whole internet first, then build by the rules.
- 2026-10-25 owner: run LIGHT jobs in parallel as many as the Mac holds (watch RAM), don't hold them back; heavy still one at a time.
- 2026-10-25 owner: act as the best compliance lawyer: read primary US laws (federal + NY first), find every weak spot a lawyer/judge/prosecutor could use, fix it in code/texts now; all legal texts editable in admin; a real lawyer reviews later when there is revenue.

### memory: research-first-workflow.md
---
name: research-first-workflow
description: "Owner's mandatory flow for every LawBid request: research how others build it, Q&A, then design, only then code"
metadata:
  node_type: memory
  type: feedback
  originSessionId: 7d021279-2390-4840-af87-dda91c51e1ba
  modified: 2026-10-22T01:56:52.855Z
---

Owner rule (2026-10-21) for every request:
1. Don't touch code first. Study the request.
2. Research how proven projects do it (GitHub, official docs, other trusted sources); look at their architecture.
3. If anything is unclear, run a question-and-answer round with the owner and get exact data.
4. Design our architecture from that, built to scale (100k → millions of users), then code.
5. If a skill / plugin / subagent from a trusted source (GitHub, official marketplaces) would make the work easier, install it myself.
6. Spend few tokens, but the result must be strong, working code.

**Why:** in the past, rushing straight into code caused bugs that took hours or days to fix; the owner doesn't want to repeat that.
**How to apply:** research → Q&A → plan → code → check → commit/merge, for every task. Related: [[owner-working-rules]].
- 2026-10-25 owner: prefer proven ready-made solutions (maintained OSS libs, AWS/Cloudflare managed features, official tools) and configure/extend them for us; write own code only where no good ready solution exists; never just code blindly. Put this in every worker brief.

### memory: scaling-principle.md
---
name: scaling-principle
description: Owner's core infra rule — cheapest start, auto up/down by usage, raisable ceilings in admin, everything ready now for split or upgrade
metadata:
  type: project
---
Owner 2026-10-24: for EVERY component (app servers, DB, pooler, Redis, search, calls, media and anything new):
- start at the cheapest size;
- grow AND shrink automatically with usage, so we never pay for empty capacity;
- the ceiling can be raised in the admin;
- the move to bigger, split or cluster setups must be prepared NOW, so later it is a switch or button in the admin (or automatic), never a code change.
**Why:** there are no users or data yet, so it is easy now and painful later.
**How to apply:**
- check new components against this rule;
- the remaining gaps are tracked in [[release-checklist-2026-10-24]]: DB split button (steps 6-10), Redis cluster-mode readiness, search switch, an admin «Масштаб» overview.
Related: [[prod-state-2026-10-24]]

The owner (2026-10-24): do NOT change the app's look in any way. That covers the layout, button positions, colors, sizes and screens. Under-the-hood work, such as calls via Cloudflare or R2, must keep every screen pixel-identical. The only allowed visible additions are ones he asked for himself, such as the outage message «На сервере идут работы…».
**Why:** he spent a long time getting the design right and is afraid it gets spoiled.
**How to apply:**
- Tell every worker touching apps/mobile or apps/admin UI: no visual changes, reuse existing widgets.
- Review diffs for layout, style or theme edits before merging.
- If a feature seems to need a UI change, ask him first.

## ▶ SECURITY REVIEW TASK (owner asked 2026-10-26 07:35)
Owner: run a defensive security review of app + web + admin + API + DB + infra — find weaknesses, fix them, repeat until two clean rounds. Authorized (owner's own product), LOCAL/staging only, never prod. Plan: review per OWASP ASVS / Top 10 dimension (authn/session, access control / IDOR, input validation & injection, file upload/storage, secrets & config, rate limits & abuse, payments/Stripe webhooks, admin 2FA, infra/IAM) → each finding gets a failing regression test, then a fix, then re-review. ORDER (owner 2026-10-26): do this LAST of everything — only after ALL PRs are merged and saved (calls, notifications/avatars, QA fixes, red-PR fixes, smoothness), right before the load tests. Not before.

## ▶ SECURITY TASK — EXPANDED SCOPE (owner 2026-10-26 07:45)
"All kinds of hacks, maximally strong, DDoS, data theft etc." — loop break→fix until every hole closed. Authorized (owner's own product), LOCAL/staging only, never prod, runs LAST after all merges.
Dimensions (each: break → failing regression test → fix → re-test, until 2 clean rounds):
1. Authn/session: token reuse/rotation, OTP abuse, enumeration, reset, session/device revocation.
2. Access control / IDOR: cross-user cases/chats/bids/payments/profiles, admin-only routes, attorney-vs-client scoping.
3. Injection: SQL (Prisma raw), NoSQL, command, template, search/blind-index, log injection.
4. Input & file upload: media type/size, SSRF via URLs, path traversal, content sniffing, Bunny/S3 signed-URL abuse.
5. Secrets & config: no secrets in repo/logs/responses, JWT strength, CORS, security headers.
6. Rate limits & DoS RESILIENCE (no real flooding): per-IP/user limits, expensive-query guards, pagination caps, resource-exhaustion, autoscale ceilings, Cloudflare/WAF, SMS/email spend caps.
7. Payments: Stripe webhook signature/replay/idempotency, amount tampering, double-charge, refund abuse.
8. Admin: 2FA on sensitive actions, infra-from-admin plan→2FA→apply safety, no-second-admin bypass, audit log.
9. Infra/IAM: least-privilege, deploy-role deny on data loss, TURN/SFU credential TTL, bucket policies.
Method: ecc:security-review + security-reviewer agents + OWASP ASVS; cite file:line; every fix = PR with regression test. NO destructive DoS against any host.
PRINCIPLE (owner 07:55): assume the attacker is an automated AI (tireless, tries everything, unfiltered) — defend systemically (no reachable surface without authorization, defense in depth, every action server-checked, no secrets client-side, abuse limits that scale) — but ZERO friction for legitimate users: owner, staff, clients, attorneys must notice nothing. Prefer invisible controls (rate limits that only automated clients hit, device binding, anomaly scoring, server-side checks) over user-facing hurdles (extra captchas, extra logins, extra approvals).

Роль workspace head (один на пространство, у финансов нет): нанимает/увольняет/выдаёт разделы только внутри своего пространства (потолок как Okta/Google OU), не меняет свои права (Zendesk), ставки/пороги/ключи/деньги — только владелец (Upwork Agency). Кнопка «обжаловать главного» → только владельцу, с анонимным вариантом (раскрытие имени — отдельное логируемое действие). Защита от мести: увольнение автора жалобы в 90 дней → красный флаг, сотрудник 14 дней в обратимом «ждёт владельца» (не блок, не four-eyes). Авто-сигналы: >15% команды за 30д (мин 5), увольнение 85+/топ-20%, без предупреждения, ≥2 жалоб от разных за 60д. Рейтинг главного: 50% качество команды, 20% текучка/обоснованность, 20% жалобы, 10% скорость очереди.
